# RedAlert Project X - map environment prompts (ChatGPT)

GENERATED by `artsrc/prompts/make_env_prompts.py` - edit that script, not this file.
Inventory: `docs/ENVIRONMENT-ASSETS.md`.

**How to use**

- Unattended: `python3 artsrc/prompts/generate_images.py --prompts env-prompts.md --category env` (add `--only trees` or similar for one group, or `--list` to preview). Images go to `assets/env/<group>/<name>.png`.
- Fewer requests: `env-sheets.md` packs related items into one image (`run_env_images.sh`).
- By hand: start a new chat for each item, attach the **Reference** image (the classic sprite, 4x enlarged) and paste the prompt, prefixed with: *"Use the attached image only as a layout reference: keep the same object, footprint, composition and camera angle, but redraw it as a high-resolution realistic asset."*
- Reject an image if the angle changes, it adds ground under an object, the background is not flat magenta, or (on two-up sheets) the two copies differ in size or position.

Total: 380 prompts.

## Map environment

**Trees** (44)

Each image: living tree (left) + burnt husk (right). The burnt half replaces the `.Husk` actor.

### 1. Tree T01 (temperate)
Save as: `trees/t01-temperate.png`
Reference: `artsrc/env-inventory/classic/TEMPERAT/t01.png`

```
Game asset sheet for a top-down real-time strategy game, showing the same tree twice, side by side in one wide image: LEFT half = the living tree, RIGHT half = the exact same tree after a fire: completely burnt, charred black trunk and bare blackened branches, no leaves. Both copies have exactly the same size, angle and position within their half, each centred in its half with a clear gap of empty background between them; nothing crosses the middle; no dividing line. The tree: a tall slender spruce with drooping branches. Setting: temperate European summer: lush green foliage, healthy natural colours. View: classic RTS three-quarter overhead view, orthographic camera looking down at about 55 degrees from the south, no perspective distortion, the whole tree visible. Its base covers a 2 x 2 square-tile footprint (width 2, depth 2) on the map. Ultra high resolution, photorealistic, highly detailed physically based materials, crisp and sharp, subtle ambient occlusion, no painterly blur. Lighting: sun from the top-left; the object casts a clearly visible soft dark shadow onto the background, falling to the bottom-right. Do not use pure bright green (#00FF00) anywhere. Isolated on a flat, solid magenta (#FF00FF) background across the whole image: no ground, no grass, no terrain base, no text, no labels, no logo, no watermark, no border. Wide image, aspect ratio 2:1.
```

```
Game asset sheet for a top-down real-time strategy game, showing the same building twice, side by side in one wide image: LEFT half = the building intact, RIGHT half = the exact same building heavily battle-damaged: scorch marks, broken windows, holes in the roof, cracked walls, rubble and small fires; still standing and clearly the same building. Isolated on a flat, solid magenta (#FF00FF) background across the whole image: no ground, no grass, no terrain base, no text, no labels, no logo, no watermark, no border. Wide image, aspect ratio 2:1.
```

**Terrain materials** (24)

Roads, cliffs, beaches, rivers, shores and interior floors are ~800 edge-matched tiles. Generating each one separately would not line up at the seams, so they are re-textured from these seamless materials, using the classic tiles as masks.

### 357. clear (temperate)
Save as: `materials/temperate-clear.png`

```
Seamless tileable ground texture for a top-down real-time strategy game: short green meadow grass with small patches of clover and bare soil. Setting: temperate European summer: lush green foliage, healthy natural colours. View: straight top-down orthographic view, camera pointing straight down, no perspective. Even, flat lighting with very soft sun from the top-left, no strong shadows, no vignette, no single dominant feature. The texture must tile seamlessly on all four edges. Ultra high resolution, photorealistic, highly detailed physically based materials, crisp and sharp, subtle ambient occlusion, no painterly blur. No text, no watermark, no border. Square image.
```
